The hook: **the shards are TRANSLUCENT stained-glass**, so the child SEES the big glow is literally MADE OF the little glows — the seams of their own tiling glow through; **part-to-whole is perceptual, not abstract.** **Build context:** 1.G.A.2/K.G.B.6 (compose composite shapes) was verified genuinely UNOWNED — the catalog NAMES (K.G.A), SORTS (1/2.G.A.1), and PARTITIONS (1/2.G.A.3) shapes but never COMPOSES them; the catalog slot was a mis-tag (line-symmetry = 4.G.A.3, above the K-2 ceiling, hard-banned from the rework). **Moods/poses:** Mim (hold-shard / **hop-cheer** / "another-way" delight / gentle not-quite tilt). ~512², transparent, 12fps.

**Props / workshop (code-drawn DOM/SVG/CSS/WebAudio/TTS; pure SVG polygons + an integer geometry kernel — NO image-library 404):** **THE BLANK LANTERN SILHOUETTE** (a single dark `<path>` / one solid filled region — **NO internal seam lines, EVER**; the tiling is the child's to discover — the anti-seam-read lock). **THE TRANSLUCENT GLASS SHARDS** (triangle + rhombus SVG `<polygon>`s on a shared integer TRIANGULAR LATTICE — cells `(r,c)`, up=`(r+c)` even, parity-preserving translation; interchangeable [a shard type fits MANY anchors → anti-jigsaw]; grey/un-fused when placed, warm/lit when the lantern flares; the seams glow through). **SELECT → TURN → tap a glowing ANCHOR to place** (tap-to-place IS the spec's a11y path + robust at 280px; commit-only auto-complete — the instant the union exactly covers the blank it FLARES, NO Check button, NO per-piece "right so far" leak; legal-but-doomed placements seat grey with no signal; free removal). **THE LANTERN SHELF** (the non-numeric vessel — a growing wall of forged lanterns; abundance, never a count) + **THE WAYS-GALLERY** (thumbnails of each DISTINCT tiling found — celebrates divergence, never a quota/rank/piece-count). **THE 6 COGNITIONS:** build / reway (the same shape a NEW piece-MULTISET — a mirror/swap of identical triangles is rejected) / recompose (the whole becomes the parts of a NEW shape) / substitute (swap a rhombus for the 2 triangles that COVER the same space — region-coverage ONLY, "half/equal-share" lexicon banned) / extend / named. **Shape-NAMES + prompts ALWAYS real DOM/SVG `<text>`** (11-locale; never baked). CA5 worklist: Mim (5 poses); stained-glass shard skins (lit + grey each — MUST keep the EXACT polygon hull the core uses, so geometry/gate stay art-independent); the flare bloom; the Lantern Shelf (fills with light). **The flare + the seams-glowing-through (the "I see the big shape is made of small ones" aha) gets the most art budget.** **PER-LOCALE = NORMAL math fan-out (geometry universal; only chrome + shape NAMES localize via the §A.13.48 native ensemble — "trapezoid/trapezium" + no clean K-1 "rhombus" in several locales is a real per-locale call). EN-only pilot.**

**A build lesson recorded:** the geometry kernel is the crux — an **integer triangular lattice** (no float in the logic; cells `(r,c)`, up=`(r+c)` even; rendering re-derives pixel vertices from integers, no drift). I avoided fragile runtime-60°-rotation by **PRECOMPUTING each piece's per-orientation cell-sets as DATA** (triangle 2 orients, rhombus 3) and letting the **backtracking composer ORACLE in the gate VERIFY them** — the oracle tiling every target 100% IS the proof the hand-authored cell-sets are geometrically correct (a wrong set → no tiling → the gate halts). The headline anti-cheat (the critic's F1) is the **GREEDY-BOUNDARY-FOLLOWER**: a child rim-walks all-triangles without composing — killed by **rationing the triangle supply below the target cell-count** so an all-triangle tiling is impossible and the interior MUST be composed into rhombi (the gate's boundary-follower solver runs out of triangles → fails every non-on-ramp build). A SECOND: the anti-jigsaw/anti-seam locks are STRUCTURAL — interchangeable pieces (a type fits many anchors) + a blank seam-free silhouette + the tap model offering ONLY legal anchors (an illegal placement is never even offered, so "the piece stayed" carries no correctness — the snap-elimination/commit-only lock). A THIRD (pilot scope): the trapezoid-PIECE + the square/half/quarter-circle curve family are DEFERRED — triangle+rhombus alone fully instantiates compose-shapes (hexagons/trapezoids/parallelograms tiled multiple ways) AND the rhombus↔2-triangles swap IS the substitute cognition; the trapezoid/hexagon are TARGET shapes composed from the two pieces. A FOURTH (probe-name collision): a DIFFERENT game already owned `_forge-probe.js` (Rivet's Number Forge) — the probe had to be `_shapeforge-probe.js` (always grep for an existing scratch-script name before writing).

## Character #41 — Inchie the inchworm & the garden path / the identical helper-worms / the object-with-a-left-notch / the garden-patch bloom vessel (Game #40 "Inchie's Garden Path — Lay the Units", 1.MD.A.2 express length as a whole number of length units laid end-to-end with no gaps/overlaps) — the run's first MEASUREMENT game (the measurement cluster's unit-iteration FOUNDATION); the integer sub-unit-lattice game

**Inchie the inchworm** measures her garden by laying a row of identical little **helper-worms** nose-to-tail along an object (a pencil, leaf, ribbon, stick…), then counting them — **the length IS the count of helpers.** The hook: there is **NO ruler, scale, tick, or endpoint number anywhere** — the child OWNS the three fused pillars of the standard (same-size units · no gaps / no overlaps, reasoned not auto-snapped · length = the count once the tiling is valid). The child taps "+ Add a helper" (it lands a step short), selects a helper, ◀▶ NUDGES it to abut, then — once the row is snug — ENUMERATES (taps each helper → it lights with its ordinal → the emergent count is the claimed length). **Build context:** 1.MD.A.2 verified genuinely UNOWNED — the live MD activities are clock (1.MD.B.3 / 2.MD.C.7), money (2.MD.C.8), and a 2.MD.A.1 ruler-READ; the `ruler.js` manipulative is free-play (ungraded); NO live activity MEASURES by iterating units. The operator chose fork A: lead the cluster with the unit-iteration foundation. **Moods/poses:** Inchie (idle / **hold-helper** / **count-along-hop** [the signature — the "length IS how many helpers" aha, hopping helper-to-helper as each lights] / gentle not-quite tilt on a gap/overlap, never a scold). ~512², transparent, 12fps.

**Props / garden (code-drawn DOM/SVG/CSS/WebAudio/TTS; pure SVG + an integer sub-unit lattice — NO image-library 404):** **THE MEASURING RAIL** (an integer lattice, `U=8` sub-steps per unit, so a 1-step gap is a ~5px sliver — gaps/overlaps stay REACHABLE + the child's to fix; **edge-snap is BANNED** — abutment is the child's act). **THE OBJECT** (a thin teal bar `[0, L·unit]` with a visual-only coral dashed **left start-notch** — the notch contributes NO snap/hit-target; the "start at the tip" pillar). **THE IDENTICAL HELPER-WORMS** (teal rounded-rect bodies + dot-eyes; the unit's pixel-width is a CORE CONSTANT — art cannot resize a helper or mixed-unit returns; a **coral gap-sliver** renders an honest gap, a **dark seam** an honest overlap — not auto-corrected; counted helpers turn coral with a white ordinal **in the worm**, never read off the object). **THE TRANSPARENT FULL-COLUMN HIT-RECT** (each tappable helper carries an invisible full-height tap band so a slim abutted unit still gives a ≥44px tap target — the "padded hit-rect even when a unit renders thin" lever). **THE ONE-ROW CONTROL STRIP** (+ Add · ◀ ▶ nudge · Take back — a single flex-wrap row so the lay phase costs ONE button row, the desktop budget). **THE COUNT IS AN ENACTED ENUMERATION — NO numeral chooser** (completion = every helper tapped exactly once → a brute-tap-count cheat is structurally impossible; a partial count never auto-accepts; a repeat-tap is a no-op). **THE GARDEN-PATCH BLOOM** (the non-numeric vessel — a flower pops + a growing garden strip; abundance, NEVER a count/score). **THE 7 COGNITIONS:** span · gaps (close it — gap = uncounted space) · overlaps (separate them — overlap = double-counting, the OPPOSITE correction) · samesize (a mixed-width row is REFUSED) · start (begin at the notch) · judge (tap the correctly-measured row among neat-but-wrong foils) · inverse (NON-graded: same object, normal vs little helpers → smaller unit, bigger count; neutral advance, no verdict). **Count-words + prompts ALWAYS real DOM/SVG `<text>`** (11-locale-ready; the "inch"-worm pun is NOT load-bearing — identical worms nose-to-tail survives any language). CA5 worklist: Inchie (idle / hold-helper / count-along-hop / not-quite tilt); the identical helper-worm (lit body / coral gap-sliver / dark overlap seam); the object-with-a-left-notch; the garden-patch bloom vessel. **The bloom + the count-along-hop (the "length IS how many helpers" aha) gets the most art budget.** **PER-LOCALE = NORMAL math fan-out (geometry/measurement universal, de-punned; localized cardinals via the §A.13.48 native ensemble, object nouns from the image-vocabulary pipeline). EN-only pilot.**

**A build lesson recorded:** the headline anti-cheat is the **BRUTE-TAP-COUNT** the critic feared — killed STRUCTURALLY by replacing a numeral chooser with an ENACTED enumeration: you cannot reach a length without tapping each distinct helper exactly once (= the actual one-to-one count), and a perfect layout with 0 helpers counted is NOT correct (the count is its OWN act, acknowledged AFTER the layout-ack). The gate's second head is **PICK-THE-NEATEST-ROW** on the judge cog — defeated by ≥2 gapless-but-wrong foils (overhang / mixed-size) within ~1 lattice step of the right length, so a low-visible-gap heuristic doesn't win. A SECOND lesson (the §A.13.62 layout crux, the bulk of the build): the real DoD gate FITS-checks the SHELL Check button (which a tall activity pushes past the fold) and tap-floors only class-matched answer CARDS, never SVG `.lu-h` sub-nodes — so the genuine fixes were VERTICAL (lower the lattice render-height `RH` 16→11 so the aspect is wide → a SHORT rail; cap the SVG max-height + KILL the desktop-enlarge rule that was the cut-off culprit; merge the two button rows into one) and the unit-WIDTH (tighten the rail margin `(L+2)→(L+1)` unit-widths so abutted units render ≥44px even at 320). A THIRD: the inverse "little" helpers are half-width by DESIGN (8 across — "smaller unit, bigger count") and can NEVER be 44px-wide — the probe must EXCLUDE them + the decorative judge sub-nodes (fix WHAT is measured when it catches intrinsically-thin/decorative nodes, never the threshold). A FOURTH (the critic's catch, fixed): the judge choice rows were SPARSE at desktop (a short worm-row floating in a full-width card) — fixed by CONTENT-SIZING the cards (`max-width` cap + center) so the worm-row fills each card; the height stays capped (3 stacked rows are height-bound at the desktop fold), so width-cap not height-bump is the lever. A FIFTH (probe-name collision, again): a different game owns `_forge-probe.js`/`_shapeforge-probe.js` — this probe is `_layunits-probe.js` (always grep before writing a scratch-script name).

## Character #42 — Pip the chocolate-forge fox & the UNLABELED mold-wall / the order box / the chunky chocolate unit-pieces / the Finished Shelf (Game #41 "Fox & Forge — Pip's Chocolate Forge", 3.NF.A.1 understand 1/b as one of b equal parts + a/b as a copies of 1/b) — the cohort's FRACTION-AS-QUANTITY entry (construct-an-amount, distinct from E14's K-2 partition recognition); the unit-iteration game

**Pip the fox** runs a chocolate forge: each order shows a fraction `a/b`, and the child fills it by recognizing the unit piece **1/b by its SIZE** (the molds are **UNLABELED** — no digit to read) and forging it `a` times into the order box, then **handing it over ONCE** (the commit is SPENT). The hook: **a/b is a copies of the unit 1/b — built, not read**; there is no pre-divided whole to shade, no fill-line, no mid-build signal — the bar EMERGES from the forged pieces, and the only path is "recognize the 1/b piece by size + iterate it." **Build context:** 3.NF.A.1 verified genuinely UNOWNED — the live E14 `fractions-core` owns only K-2 geometric-partition RECOGNITION (1.G.A.3/2.G.A.3/2.G.A.2; a child can pass it while still believing "1/8 > 1/4 because 8 > 4"); the also-approved Game 76 ("Shard-Smiths") is the DISTINCT symbol-first predict-then-reveal sibling (both ship, zero overlap). Operator chose Fork A: re-tag the catalog's `2.G.A.3` mis-tag → 3.NF.A.1, build-as-specced with the "perfect each order" spent-commit. **Moods/poses:** Pip (idle / forging / happy-wiggle / gentle taste-check; never fails the child). ~512², transparent, 12fps.

**Props / forge (code-drawn DOM/SVG/CSS/WebAudio/TTS; pure SVG + a unit-size kernel — NO image-library 404):** **THE ORDER BOX** (the whole bar, an empty outline; the child taps the empty remainder to FORGE a 1/b piece, taps a forged piece to UN-FORGE — pieces abut left-to-right, the bar fills a/b of the way; NO fill-line, nothing changes at count===a). **THE UNLABELED MOLD-WALL** (≥2 mold buttons, each a faint full-bar outline + the 1/b unit piece at the left, at the SAME scale across molds so the SIZE compares — **no printed denominator, ever**; the correct mold + plausible foils, **distractor denominators clearly SPACED** [the critic lesson: adjacent denominators like 1/6 vs 1/8 are ~3px apart and unjudgeable — foils must be 0.5×/2×/4× different so the size-judgment is solvable]; one round offers a CRACKED "unequal" mold of visibly-mismatched chunks that the **forge REFUSES** [the "1/b needs EQUAL parts" consequence]). **THE "HAND IT OVER" COMMIT** (a deliberate spent commit — a wrong hand-over clears the box, no count-probing the same order). **THE a/b FRACTION CHIP** (real DOM `<text>`, stacked numerator/bar/denominator; aria = the spoken fraction "three fourths", never "3 slash 4"). **THE TWO-BAR the-whole round** (a small reference bar + a bigger active bar, both a/b — the size difference is the "the whole matters" point). **THE NAME-UNIT round** (the bar in b equal parts, ONE shaded → tap 1/b among fraction chips). **THE CROSS-SHAPE round** (the bar half + the pizza half → affirm same-share; non-scored). **THE FINISHED SHELF** (the non-numeric abundance vessel — a growing strip of chocolate bars; never a count). **THE DONE STATE renders the COMPLETED a/b bar** (the critic lesson: an emoji + text alone reads SPARSE at desktop — show the finished order so the card is full). **THE CANONICAL UNIT PIXEL-SIZE per b is a CORE CONSTANT** (art can never resize a piece, or the denominator stops being load-bearing). CA5 worklist: Pip (4 poses); the chocolate slab + pizza-wedge unit skins (sized from b, NO baked label); the UNLABELED mold sprites + the cracked unequal-mold variant; the Finished Shelf; 5-6 forest customers. **The forge + the hand-over + the Finished Shelf filling (the "a/b is a copies of 1/b" aha — built by iterating the unit) gets the most art budget.** **PER-LOCALE = math fan-out with a NATIVE-ENSEMBLE fraction-WORD layer (NOT an a/b template — suppletive: half/Hälfte/moitié/mitad/metà; 1/3 Drittel/tiers/tercio; gender/agreement trois quarts m / una cuarta parte; §A.13.48). EN-only pilot.**

**A build lesson recorded:** the headline anti-cheat is **MOLD-MATCH** (digit-match the denominator → the tool enforces unit-correctness for free) — killed STRUCTURALLY by UNLABELED molds (no digit to match → the child MUST judge size) + reachable wrong-forgeable foils; the gate's mold-matcher lands on a wrong forgeable mold → the unit-size gate (every placed piece size===1/b) rejects. The second head is **COMMIT-PROBER** (hand over at counts 1,2,3 seeking acceptance) — killed by commit-is-SPENT (a wrong hand-over clears the box; one shot per order). Plus COUNT-THE-NUMERATOR (right count, wrong size → wrong-size-piece), GLOW-CHASER (no mid-build signal → fills the bar = over), UNEQUAL-PARTS (the forge refuses the cracked mold), OVERFILL, MIXED-SIZE — all ≤ chance, oracle 100%. A SECOND lesson (the critic's catch, the SHIP-BLOCKER): on an UNLABELED size-judgment task **the distractor denominators must be clearly SPACED** — adjacent denominators (1/6 vs 1/8) render ~3px apart and the core cognition becomes unsolvable by eye; re-spacing the foils to 0.5×/2×/4× + making the mold pieces taller/chunkier (there is free vertical room inside the 46px button) makes the size-judgment legible. A THIRD (chrome budget): the two-bar the-whole round + the long-prompt equal-parts round are the tall states — cap the two-bar SVG shorter (`.ff-svg2`, and the `≤380` media query must ALSO cap it or it re-inflates) + trim the longest prompts so the shell Check clears the fold at 320×640 AND 1024×900. A FOURTH (done-state): a bare emoji+text done splash reads SPARSE at desktop — render the COMPLETED a/b bar so the card is full (abundance, the finished order). A FIFTH (probe-name): "forge" is heavily used (`_forge-probe.js` Rivet's Number Forge, `_shapeforge-probe.js` Game 39) — this game is `fox-forge` / `_foxforge-probe.js` (grep before writing).

## Character #43 — Lily the frog & the 0–20 number line / the golden Ten Stone / the make-ten bond frame / the pond + dragonfly bloom vessel (Game #43 "Lily's Ten Stones", 1.OA.C.6 add & subtract within 20 with the make-ten strategy) — the cohort's NUMBER-LINE make-ten game (strategy-not-fluency, the spatial bridge)

**Lily the frog** crosses a 0–20 number-line pond by the MAKE-TEN strategy: for `8 + 6`, the child fills the always-visible bond frame **"8 + ⬚ = 10"** on a `[−] (n) [+]` spinner — the declared part DRIVES the hop to the **golden Ten Stone** at 10 — then fills the remainder **"10 + ⬚ = ?"** and hops to the target. **Subtraction "hops back home"** (13−4 leftward). The hook: **the make-ten decomposition IS the spatial path** — strategy, not a speed drill; the answer numeral is HIDDEN until Lily lands. **Build context (the GATE-CHECK CORRECTION):** the INDEX's "UNOWNED" note was STALE — the live free **E18 number-bond** ships `make-ten-to-add` (1.OA.C.6, a BOND diagram) doing the make-ten-ADD spine; **operator ruled "build as-specced"** (the Game-38 precedent) because the NUMBER-LINE representation + the 4 unowned cognitions (subtraction bridge-DOWN, choose-anchor, near-double, find-where-ten) carry it. Operator fork B1 (the bond frame always visible, declared pre-hop). **Moods/poses:** Lily (idle / mid-hop / made-ten-happy / waiting-patient). ~512², transparent, 12fps.

**Props / pond (code-drawn DOM/SVG/CSS/WebAudio/TTS; pure SVG + the make-ten kernel — NO image-library 404):** **THE 0–20 NUMBER LINE** (labels 0/5/10/15/20; the golden Ten Stone at 10 — the only mid-pond rest; value→pct mirrors track-repair's `valueToPct`, RE-implemented 0-lines-to-it). **THE BOND FRAME + SPINNER** ("N + ⬚ = 10"; the child DECLARES the to-ten part; the declared part BECOMES the hop). **THE HOP COMMIT** (each HOP commits the declared bond; a bond that misses ten rolls back — warm, no land, no red; there is NO per-hop signal and NO block-gradient → the spin-to-ten cheat has nothing to search). **THE HOP ARCS** (coral "+2"/"+4" drawn on each successful hop — the make-ten bridge visualized 8 →+2→ 10 →+4→ 14). **THE EQUATION CHIP** ("8 + 6 = ?" — the answer HIDDEN until Lily lands, then revealed). **THE CHOOSE ROUNDS** (find-where-ten yes/no; choose-the-anchor; near-double). **THE RELATION REVEAL** (non-graded: 8+6=14 → 14−6=8 fact family). **THE POND/DRAGONFLY bloom vessel** (non-numeric abundance). **Numbers/equation/bond ALWAYS real DOM/SVG `<text>`** (11-locale; SR reads natural cardinals). CA5 worklist: Lily (4 poses); the golden Ten Stone (ordinary + bloom); the pond/banks; the dragonfly + lily-pad vessel; the hop-arc texture. **The made-ten bloom + the "8 and 2 make ten, rest on the golden stone" aha gets the most art budget.** **PER-LOCALE = math fan-out (numbers carry it; the "make ten / rest on the stone / hop back home" callouts + localized CARDINALS native-authored; subtraction-direction `[NSR-FLAG]` Nordic). EN-only pilot.**

**A build lesson recorded — THE GATE-CHECK-FIRST DISCIPLINE (the Game-59 stale-note pattern):** the INDEX's "UNOWNED" prose was STALE; the LIVE E18 number-bond ALREADY ships `make-ten-to-add` (1.OA.C.6, 11-locale) — **always verify a candidate standard against the LIVE free activities + the already-designed `game-designs/*.md`, NOT the policy-note prose**. Surfaced as a business fork via AskUserQuestion (Games 38/59 precedent); operator ruled "build as-specced" since only 1 of 5 graded cognitions overlaps + the number-line representation + subtraction-bridge + meta-strategy are distinct. A SECOND lesson — the headline **SPIN-TO-TEN-SEARCHER** is killed STRUCTURALLY by the operator-B1 fix: the hop IS the declared bond, and `evaluate` returns a CATEGORICAL `wrong-to-ten` with NO proximity info → no gradient to search (the gate proves a wrong first bond returns the SAME status regardless of closeness). A THIRD (layout): a number-line game is HORIZONTAL → the SVG is short → fits the §A.13.62 budget comfortably (the probe passed first try). A FOURTH: the answer is "?" until Lily LANDS — even knowing the target a solver must declare the make-ten bonds (toTen=10−start, remainder=target−10), which IS the strategy. A FIFTH (probe-name): grepped clear (`_tenstones-probe.js`).

## Character #44 — Snippy the Sound-Spark Snail & the trace paper / the start-dots / the faded guide-path / the garden-bloom vessel (Game #44 "Snippy's Letter Trace", L.K.1.a print upper- & lowercase LETTERS — letter FORMATION) — the cohort's first LETTER-FORMATION game + the first EN-only-by-design game; the draw-trace handwriting engine

**Snippy the snail's ink trail IS the letter.** The child FORMS a shown letter by stroke: **GUIDED first** (the faded dotted stroke-path + a numbered coral start-dot + a "trace along" chip — practice), then the **GRADED CERTIFY pass** (the path + order numbers HIDDEN; only the **unlabeled, unordered coral start-dots** + an "on your own" chip — the child picks the CORRECT start among all starts + takes the strokes in order from memory). A wrong certify start gently DEMOTES to a re-peek (no free retry). The hook: **the letter is SHOWN (formation, NOT recognition — the sound/word is a light frame "Trace c · cat"); a dot-follower with no path + no order must KNOW the letter.** **Build context (the gate-check):** letter FORMATION (L.K.1.a) verified genuinely UNOWNED — Game 21 "Mama's Roll Call" (`numeral-trace-core.js`) traces NUMERALS only (K.CC.B.5); the cvc/word-builder are letter ASSEMBLY (tapping tiles); the choice-board is letter RECOGNITION. The bright line = PRODUCTION vs RECOGNITION. Operator fork A1 (claim L.K.1.a fully). **Moods/poses:** Snippy (idle-hum / ride-the-stroke / happy-bloom / "let's-practice" gentle). ~512², transparent, 12fps.

**Props / paper (code-drawn DOM/SVG/CSS/WebAudio/TTS; pure SVG stroke geometry — NO image-library 404):** **THE TRACE PAPER** (a square SVG; pointerdown near a start-dot → pointermove draws the ink trail → pointerup completes the stroke; the path geometry models `numeral-trace-core.js`'s ordered checkpoint arrays in a 0..100 box, RE-implemented 0-lines-to-it). **THE START-DOTS** (per-stroke; at GUIDED the next start is a numbered coral pin + the faded dotted guide-path is shown; at CERTIFY all starts are UNLABELED, UNORDERED coral dots + NO path/numbers — the load-bearing difference; the start hit-radius is a generous START_TOL so a K finger lands). **THE 3-LEVEL PASS** (guided → checkpoint → CERTIFY; only certify is GRADED — the cosmetic-fade fix). **THE WORD-ANCHOR CHIP** ("Trace c · cat" — the letter SHOWN + a light word frame; never a graded recognition gate). **THE COMMITTED INK** (the child's completed strokes stay teal; the in-drag is coral). **THE DONE PAPER** (the child's FORMED letter rendered as their ink + the bloom — the "you formed THIS letter" payoff, not a sparse card). **THE GARDEN-ROW bloom vessel** (s→sunflower; growth, never a count). **The letter glyph + the word ALWAYS real DOM/SVG `<text>`** (the trace path is SVG; 11-locale-ready). CA5 worklist: Snippy (5 poses); the bloom plants (one per first-wave letter — sun/moon/apple/top…); the garden-row vessel; the ink-trail texture; optional per-letter native /phoneme/ clips (a pure later enhancement — ships + gates on word-TTS). **The bloom + the ride-the-stroke formation aha gets the most art budget.** **PER-LOCALE = EN-ONLY pilot, each locale a SEPARATE §21.3 native commission** (native letter inventory — **ß EXCLUDED**, no uppercase/no K-handwriting form; å/ä/æ/ø/ñ/ü diacritic strokes native-authored each with its own start+order; a native K-frequency word-anchor per letter, never a translated keyword).

**A build lesson recorded — the CERTIFYING-PASS + the CONVENTION-FREE HOMER:** the headline anti-cheat is the **CHECKPOINT-HOMING-FOLLOWER** (a convention-free GEOMETRIC dot-connector — it KNOWS the checkpoint coords but has ZERO handwriting knowledge, so it orders strokes by PROXIMITY-to-origin and traces nearest-neighbour). It is defeated at the certify START-SELECTION: the certify deck is SKEWED so the formation-first stroke is NOT the proximity-nearest one for the MULTI-STROKE MAJORITY (i/j: the dot is drawn LAST but sits nearest the origin; t: the cross is nearer than the stem-top; a/d: the loop is drawn before the stick but the stick is nearer; f: the cross is nearer than the hook) → the homer picks the WRONG first start → demoted. Measured: the homer certifies only 20% (defeated on 80%); a child who knows the letter forms 100%. The **smartest convention-free cheat fails; granting it the top-down rule would be granting the very formation knowledge under test** (so the convention-free homer is the honest ceiling). A SECOND lesson — at certify a bare start-TAP must form NOTHING (a real trace is REQUIRED), else the dot-follower passes single-stroke letters by tapping the lone start; the fix: certify with a null/empty path → `off-path` (the dot-follower scores 0%). A THIRD (the cosmetic-fade fix): the GUIDED scaffold (path + numbered starts) and the CERTIFY surface (no path, unlabeled starts) must be VISIBLY different — the chip even flips green "trace along" → coral "on your own"; the gate asserts the certify snapshot carries NO glyphPath + NO order numbers while guided carries both. A FOURTH (done-state, the fox-forge lesson re-applied): render the child's FORMED letter (their ink) in the done state so it's the accomplishment payoff, not a sparse flower-only card (the `.sn-donepaper` must be flex so the `width:auto` SVG doesn't collapse). A FIFTH: the first EN-only-by-design game in the series (non-EN 404; per-locale = native commissions); grepped "snippy"/"letterform"/"sound-spark" clear before writing (`_snippy-probe.js`).

## Character #45 — Tildy the tailor & Knot the cat / the draggable ruler / the strip + read-line / the wrapped-parcels Finished-Orders shelf (Game #46 "Tildy's Tailor Shop — Ruler Lab", 2.MD.A.1 measure length by USING a ruler) — the measurement cluster's read-a-scale member; the child-positioned-ruler game (alignment is the load-bearing act)

**Tildy the tailor** measures ribbons + cords with a ruler; **Knot the cat** keeps nudging the ruler off-zero (the diegetic excuse for the rule: the CHILD positions it). The child DRAGS the ruler so its **0 lines up with the strip's START**, presses **"Measure it"** (the binary POST-COMMIT check), then taps the cm the far edge reads. The hook: **the ALIGNMENT is the load-bearing act** — the reading is a PURE FUNCTION of placement (the numerals slide WITH the ruler → mis-align → wrong number), there is **NO live "green when aligned" lamp** (feedback only AFTER the commit), and the **off-by-one** (the 0 one cm into the strip → reads ONE SHORT) is reachable + shown as a warm consequence. **Build context (the gate-check):** the final read-numeral OVERLAPS the LIVE free `choice-board.read-ruler.2-md-a-1` (an image-READ that PRE-ALIGNS the ruler at 0); **operator ruled "build as-specced"** (the Games 38/41/43 precedent) — this game's load-bearing cognition is the child POSITIONING the ruler (the off-by-one) + broken-ruler span + fix-by-diagnosis + select-tool, none of which the pre-aligned read-ruler owns. Operator Fork A (cm-pilot). **Moods/poses:** Tildy (idle / measuring / happy-wrap / "let's-check" gentle) + Knot (nudge). ~512², transparent, 12fps.

**Props / shop (code-drawn DOM/SVG/CSS/WebAudio/TTS; pure SVG + an integer cm lattice — NO image-library 404):** **THE DRAGGABLE RULER** (ticks + numerals as real `<text>`; a prominent coral 0-mark; pointer-drag, snaps to integer cm so the off-by-one lands cleanly on the adjacent integer; models the free `ruler.js` geometry + lay-units' lattice, 0-lines-to-them). **THE STRIP** (ribbon/leather/yarn — a teal bar with a coral START-tick; carries NO baked number). **THE READ-LINE** (coral dashed, fixed to the strip's FAR edge — carries no number of its own; the numerals slide with the ruler → the reading is relative to placement). **THE "MEASURE IT" COMMIT** (the ONLY eval moment — NO live lamp during dragging → a nudge-to-green searcher has no gradient; a mis-aligned commit shows the consequence, not a green). **THE BROKEN RULER** (origin relabeled from "2" → count the cm-marks the strip COVERS, never subtract). **THE DIAGNOSE round** (a shown wrong measurement → tap WHY: "Started at 1" / "Too short" / "Wrong end"). **THE SELECT-TOOL round** (a too-short ruler runs out → pick a tool long enough to reach). **THE WRAPPED-PARCELS Finished-Orders shelf** (the non-numeric abundance vessel). **Numerals ALWAYS real DOM/SVG `<text>`** (the true length is NEVER rendered until correct). CA5 worklist: Tildy (4 poses); Knot (nudge); the ruler/tape/yardstick/broken-ruler tool sprites (numerals stay live `<text>`); the ribbon/leather/yarn/vertical-cord strip skins; the wrapped parcels + bow + shelf. **The wrap-and-shelve + the off-by-one consequence reveal get the most art budget.** **PER-LOCALE = cm for the EN pilot (the international-school audience = metric); the engine is unit-AGNOSTIC; per-locale unit table at fan-out (en-US → inches, metric → cm; an 8-inch ribbon ≠ a 20-cm ribbon = different content). EN pilot ships cm.**

**A build lesson recorded — THE NO-LIVE-LAMP / NUDGE-TO-GREEN fix (the Game-43 search killed again):** the headline anti-cheat is the **NUDGE-TO-GREEN-SEARCHER** (a child who hill-climbs on a live "green when aligned" lamp — a Game-43-spin-to-ten-analog search the gate is blind to). Killed STRUCTURALLY: NO live lamp → a binary POST-COMMIT "Measure it" check + `aria-valuetext` narrates the POSITION of two landmarks ("zero is at the 3-cm line; the ribbon starts at the 5-cm line") NOT proximity ("warmer") → there is NO gradient to climb; the gate proves it (every WRONG placement returns the SAME categorical `zero-not-at-start` status → no monotone distance signal). The child must IDENTIFY the start (a committed assertion). Plus AUTO-ALIGN-READER (reads the Knot-nudged unplaced ruler → not aligned), OFF-BY-ONE (the 0 one cm in → reads one short, a clean adjacent integer shown post-commit), MIS-ALIGN, ANSWER-READER (no stored length; aria names only the landmark positions), WRONG-TOOL — all ≤ chance, oracle 100%. A SECOND lesson (the stale-INDEX gate-check, the Game-59 pattern): the spec's "UNOWNED" claim missed the LIVE `choice-board.read-ruler` (a pre-aligned image-READ) — **always verify against the LIVE free activities, not the spec's prose**; surfaced via AskUserQuestion → operator "build as-specced" (production/position-the-tool vs recognition/read-a-pre-aligned-image; only the read-numeral overlaps). A THIRD (layout): the diagnose round's 3 stacked chips were the tall state → row-wrap + short labels ("Started at 1" / "Too short" / "Wrong end") packed them into one row at 320×640. A FOURTH (done-state): render only the win — don't leave the round's prompt above the celebration (the critic's flag). A FIFTH: grepped "tildy"/"rulerlab"/"tailor" clear before writing (`_tildy-probe.js`); appended this character via a temp-file `cat` (the `<<HEREDOC` form silently fails in this shell).

## Character #46 — Bramble the badger / the two mismatched containers / the berry-juice pour (overflow-spill / exact-brim sparkle / leaves-room) / the woven berry basket (Game #47 "Bramble's Berry-Juice Stand", K.MD.A.2 directly compare two objects by CAPACITY — which holds MORE/LESS/the SAME) — the measurement cluster's NON-VISUAL-attribute member; the predict-then-verify conservation game (the COMMITTED PREDICTION is the load-bearing act)

**Bramble the badger** runs a woodland berry-juice stand and can never remember which of his mismatched cups holds more — **his eyes lie to him about tall ones**. The child is his Pouring Pal: *call it before he pours.* The hook: **you can't SEE which holds more** (a tall-skinny glass LOOKS like more than a short-wide bowl but may hold LESS — the conservation illusion), so the child COMMITS a more/less/same claim (3 buttons: Left / Right / Same) **BEFORE any pour**, then the pour transfers A→B and VERIFIES — **OVERFLOWS** (A held more) / **EXACT BRIM + sparkle** (same) / **LEAVES ROOM** (B held more). **Grade the PREDICTION; the pour is the standard's "directly compare," NOT a cheat.** **Build context (the gate-check / §22.1 same-code-different-mechanic):** a LIVE free `choice-board.compare-length.k-md-2` ("Comparing Length", tap taller/shorter/longer) already claims K.MD.A.2 — but on the VISUAL LENGTH attribute (tap-the-taller recognition). Game 47 is the **NON-VISUAL CAPACITY** attribute (predict-before-pour conservation). The spec AND the INDEX both already documented this as "distinct from E2's visual length" (NOT a stale note like Game 46); **operator ruled "build as-specced"** (the Games 38/41/43/46 precedent). **Moods/poses:** Bramble neutral-curious (predict) / gasp-eyes-fooled (illusion miss) / delight (correct) / trusts-and-right (clear-cut on-ramp) — **sometimes-RIGHT-sometimes-fooled** (modeling the real cognition "appearance is SOMETIMES trustworthy — decide per round," NOT a dumb always-distrust-height invert). ~512², transparent, 12fps.

**Props / stand (code-drawn DOM/SVG/CSS/WebAudio/TTS; pure SVG silhouettes + a `transform:scaleY` liquid tween — NO canvas/physics/fluid-sim, "gate information, don't simulate liquid"; NO image-library 404):** **THE TWO CONTAINERS** drawn in ONE shared SVG coordinate space (so the relative height/width contrast is HONEST — never per-box auto-fit). Stub = a tapered beaker silhouette parameterized by `drawnHeight`/`drawnWidth`; the named vessels (glass / bowl / jug / bottle / tub / vase / pan / dish / crate / cup / pipe / flute / tower / pot) get distinct hulls in CA5. **THE JUICE POUR** (a clipped `<rect>` scaleY 0→1: cup A fills, then A empties as B fills to the OUTCOME level — overflow=brim+a coral **spill glyph**, exact=brim+a gold **sparkle**, room=~55% partial; the level is set by the categorical outcome, NOT a volume sim). **THE WOVEN BERRY BASKET** (the non-numeric abundance vessel — a berry drops on EVERY resolve, right or wrong; sparse→heaped, NEVER a count). **THE PREDICT BUTTONS** (Left / Right / Same with container-tint dots; the fit round swaps to FITS / OVERFLOWS). **Words ALWAYS real DOM/SVG `<text>`** (more/less/same; 11-locale native-authored at fan-out; NEVER baked). **NO trueCapacity / liquid level / numeral / unit / line is EVER in the pre-commit DOM** (the result is decoupled from the drawn shape AND withheld until commit — both motion modes). CA5 worklist: Bramble (4 poses); the ~14 vessel hulls matching the SVG silhouettes; the juice-fill + overflow-spill + exact-brim sparkle; the berry + the woven basket (sparse→heaped). **The pour-reveal + the eyes-fooled gasp get the most art budget.** **PER-LOCALE = EN pilot** (the more/less/same words + the describe-sentence are a §A.13.48 native ensemble at fan-out, "same" anchored to a fixed neutral quantity noun "the same amount" — NEVER a `{word}` token, the §A.13.56 Finnish trap); full 11-locale eligible; `K.MD.A.2` the machine/SEO anchor.

**Build lessons recorded — (1) THE APPEARANCE-DECOUPLED DECK (the gate's first FAIL → content fix, never a threshold move):** the conservation illusion is honestly "no 2D silhouette dimension tells you volume," so the deck must defeat JUDGE-BY-HEIGHT **and** JUDGE-BY-WIDTH **and** JUDGE-BY-AREA — not just height. My first deck had every illusion as "the wider holds more" → the width+area solvers scored 100% and the measured gate FAILED. The fix was to add the width-misleading direction (a `narrow-wins` cog = the tall-NARROW holds MORE) + full-`decouple` rounds (the bigger-LOOKING holds LESS), balancing the assessed body to ≤38%/≤38%/0% for height/width/area while keeping ≥60% height-anti-correlation (the §A.13.62 rule: **fix the content so the gate passes honestly; NEVER weaken the gate**). This also models F9 (appearance is sometimes trustworthy — decide per round). **(2) THE STATEFUL MEMORIZE-EXCEPTIONS GATE (the critic's deepest fix):** a "trust appearance unless this exact pair fooled me, then flip" cheat passes via pair-MEMORIZATION (zero conservation) — and a stateless gate is blind to it. Defeated STRUCTURALLY: every re-test (`transfer` cog, `reofferOf` a fooled structure) is a **NOVEL surface of the SAME structure** (different shapes/proportions; same `structuralSignature`), and no surface ever repeats — so a surface-keyed memory cache NEVER hits → the memorizer has no edge over the plain height solver (both ≤ chance). The gate now ships a STATEFUL adversary (caches `surfaceKey→answer`, must FAIL) + asserts `reofferUsesNovelSurfaceSameStructure` + `noPairRepeatsIdentically` — closing the stateless-only blind spot (generalizes to every cohort re-offer mechanic). **(3) NO-LIVE-LAMP / PREDICT-FIRST:** `playPour()` THROWS before a prediction is committed (the result is structurally unreachable pre-commit); `snapshot()` carries shape + geometry ONLY (no capacity/level/numeral) on both render modes → there is no gradient to hill-climb and nothing to read. **(4) WARM-ADVANCE-ON-MISS → the lcs-shell Check mapping (the design's "no Check button"):** the 3 predict buttons are the COMMIT; the shell `.lcs-activity-check` is the post-pour "Serve the guest" confirm, **hidden until the round resolves** via a `.lcs-app.bramble-resolved` marker class (0 lines to lcs-shell.js/.css). The round resolves — and the shell celebrate fires — REGARDLESS of correctness; a miss is a warm in-stage discovery (Bramble: "My eyes lied to both of us — the WIDE one held more! Now we know!", coral, no red-X), a berry STILL drops, and the fooled structure returns later on a fresh pair. The grade lives in-core (`isCorrect`, what the gate scores) + in Bramble's reaction, never in a shell try-again. **(5) THE STALE-TIMEOUT TOKEN GUARD (a real latent bug the "personally Read" step caught):** the describe round schedules a deferred `_toDescribe`; a Reset/advance mid-pour could let that prior-round timeout fire and corrupt the new round's phase (a phone capture literally showed the describe prompt bleed onto a wide-wins round). Fixed with a per-round `_token` incremented in `_beginRound`; every deferred pour/describe/resolve callback bails if the token changed. **(6) THE CRITIC'S READABILITY NIT → the stimulus IS the cognition:** the first stub drew the containers as small thin glyphs floating in a wide empty band; the conservation contrast is the whole point, so I tightened the viewBox + enlarged the vessels + thickened the stroke (a layout fix, gate-revalidated, not an art deferral). **(7) the temp-file `cat` append** (NOT a `<<HEREDOC` — it silently fails in this shell). Architecture: a NEW clean `bramble-core.js`/`bramble-activity.js` (window.Bramble*), **0 lines to any protected core / lcs-shell** (the only tracked edit = the `bramble-activities.json` row in `activities.ts MANIFEST_FILES`); the gate trio `verify-bramble-core.js` (MEASURED) + `_bramble-probe.js` + `local-test-bramble.js`; full §A.13.62 DoD green (core gate + visual-qa 78 renders + probe + local-test + personal Read 768/1024/360 + visual-critic PASS-WITH-NITS, nit fixed). No deploy (operator batches).

## Character #47 — Pip the forager & the Stacking Fence (a build-by-hand bar graph) / the tally sheet + survey slips / the meadow creatures / the woven Harvest Basket (Game #48 "Pip's Stacking Fence / Graph Grove", 2.MD.D.10 draw a picture/bar graph for ≤4 categories + solve put-together/take-apart/COMPARE problems from it) — the data/graphs FAMILY-OPENER; the build-AND-read game (TWO load-bearing verbs)

**⚠️ NAME REUSE FLAG (operator decision):** the spec names this forager **"Pip"**, but **character #42 is already "Pip the chocolate-forge fox"** (Game 41). This build ships as "Pip" per the spec; the operator should rule whether the two are the SAME recurring Pip (then unify the art) or a DISTINCT forager who needs a different name (the badger-forager here vs the fox-chocolatier there). Not a blocker — the stub uses a generic woodland-forager SVG (a small fox/badger with a teal scarf); CA5 resolves the identity.

**Pip the forager builds a Stacking Fence — a bar graph of slatted pickets, one slot per gathered thing** — because the meadow creatures lose track of who gathered how many. The child is Pip: build the fence, then read the answer right off it. **TWO load-bearing verbs (the standard has both):** **(1) REPRESENT** — the child BUILDS the graph by tapping a category COLUMN to stack one unit (the bar grows BY HAND; the graph starts EMPTY; an Undo removes the last; auto-resolves on the EXACT per-category count — a column-swap or off-by-one just doesn't resolve, adjust freely against the visible tally, no fail). **(2) INTERPRET** — a RELATIONAL read OFF the graph (how-many-more / fewer / total / a true-false verify), **NEVER "which is most"** (the perceptual floor — not shipped). On compare rounds the two referenced bars are CLOSE and a THIRD bar is tallest, so eyeballing/read-tallest fails. **Build context (the gate-check):** a clean strand-opener — `2.MD.D.10` + `1.MD.C.4` are UNOWNED at the activity level (the `chart-count` worksheet is a deck/PDF generator with no activity; the closest relational neighbor `friendship-bridge` is K.CC.C.6 — a different standard/grade/mechanic, direct two-row compare not graph-build-and-read). NOT a same-CCSS-code overlap → no operator ruling needed; proceeded as approved. **Moods/poses:** Pip idle / happy-hop (a correct read → a gathered thing drops into the Harvest Basket) / "let's re-read" scaffold pose. ~512², transparent, 12fps.

**Props / grove (code-drawn DOM/SVG/CSS/WebAudio/TTS; pure SVG + an integer-count kernel — NO canvas/physics, NO image-library 404):** **THE STACKING FENCE** = a vertical bar graph, one slatted column per category (per-unit stacked cells; faint gridlines at 5 + 10; categories distinguished by SHAPE + LABEL + colour, never hue alone — leaf/berry/acorn/pinecone/mushroom/flower). **THE TALLY SHEET / SURVEY SLIPS** (the data to decode: `‖‖‖‖` grouped in 5s with a coloured 5th-mark; or 1:1 icons for the picture-build on-ramp). **THE ANSWER RAIL** (integer chips around the answer, or TRUE/FALSE for verify) + the coral **"That's my answer!" DELIBERATE COMMIT** (NOT a tap-to-answer — the deliberate commit is the anti-brute-tap guard). **THE GUIDED RE-READ** (a wrong interpret commit highlights the TWO referenced bars in coral + Pip speaks the count-up scaffold "Leaves has 6, Berries has 4 — count up from Berries" + the rail RESHUFFLES — warm, never a red-X). **THE MEADOW CREATURES** (rabbit/fox/bird/mouse — idle + hop on a correct read). **THE WOVEN HARVEST BASKET** (the non-numeric abundance vessel; a berry drops on every solve; sparse→bountiful, NEVER a count). **A visually-hidden live `<table>` MIRROR** (category+count rows; the SVG plot `aria-hidden`) gives SR/keyboard equivalence on BOTH verbs (it updates as the child builds). **Numbers ALWAYS real DOM/SVG `<text>`** (the single-unit axis 0..10, the answers; 11-locale; NEVER baked; NO multi-unit scale — that's Grade 3). CA5 worklist: Pip (idle/hop/scaffold — 3 poses, pending the name ruling); the meadow creatures; clean stackable token sprites (acorn/leaf/berry/pinecone — numbers stay DOM); the slatted Stacking-Fence frame (decorative; bars stay SVG geometry); the Harvest Basket (sparse→bountiful). **The build-the-fence + the guided-reread "I read it off the graph" aha get the most art budget.** **PER-LOCALE = EN pilot;** the relational phrasings ("how many more/fewer/total than", the category-label agreement) are a §21.3 new-content-type → §A.13.48 native ensemble at fan-out (the §A.13.56 comparative-agreement trap — Finnish partitive, Romance gender, "more X than Y" order — NEVER machine-interpolated).

**Build lessons recorded — (1) THE BRUTE_TAPPER GATE ADVERSARY (the critic's deepest fix + a PROGRAM-WIDE finding):** a small-answer-space interpret is brute-tappable — a child enumerates the answer chips until one is accepted, bypassing the graph-read, and the cohort's measured gates test only COMPUTING solvers (blind to the ENUMERATOR). Defeated structurally: the INTERPRET answer requires a DELIBERATE COMMIT (select → commit, not tap-to-answer); a wrong commit routes through the guided re-read (the two bars highlight + a spoken scaffold + the rail reshuffles + the selection clears) so every wrong submission is a graph-read, not a free retry. The gate now ships a `BRUTE_TAPPER` adversary — (a) no BLIND first-commit strategy (constant-0 / constant-max / tallest-count / one-bar) beats the answer-distribution spread (≤34%, measured), AND (b) enumerate-until-accepted needs >1 try on average (can't first-try without reading) — plus the structural flags (`interpretRequiresDeliberateCommit` + `wrongCommitRoutesGuidedReread` + `answerFromGraphNotStored`, the last proven by mutating the graph and watching `expectedAnswer` track it). **TECH-DEBT FILED (program-wide): retrofit the BRUTE_TAPPER enumerator adversary to Games 40/43 + any small-answer-space game.** **(2) THE APPEARANCE-PROOF DECK (read-tallest + count-one-bar = 0%):** every compare round keeps the two referenced bars CLOSE (gap ≤3) with a THIRD bar strictly tallest, and the answer never equals either operand or the tallest — so read-tallest, count-one-bar, and auto-tally (the graph starts empty → `builtMatchesData([])` false) all score 0; the oracle that builds + reads relationally wins 100%. The first deck FAILED the gate twice (a compare gap of 4 broke close-bars; the match round had no `question.type` so `expectedAnswer` returned null) — **fix the content/data, never the gate** (narrowed the gap, which also improved the answer spread; added the match question type). **(3) THE QUESTION RIDES IN THE SHELL PROMPT (chrome budget):** the relational question is the lcs-shell prompt (promptKey + promptArgs → free TTS speaker), not duplicated in-stage — saving a line so the graph + rail + commit fit the §A.13.62 fold. **(4) THE VISUAL-QA "appH FALLBACK" (fix WHAT is measured, never the threshold):** the visual-qa gate reported a desktop CUT-OFF (927>900) because it couldn't match my `.gi-chip`/`.gi-btn` controls to its card selector (`-card`/`-choice`/`-cand`) and fell back to the whole-`.lcs-app` height (the `height:100%` fill + trailing empty shell chrome) — but the SCREENSHOT proved the content fit with margin. The honest fix (per the §A.13.62 note) was to make the gate measure my REAL controls — added a `gi-card` class to the answer chips/commit/undo/thumbs (they ARE the "big tappable choices" the selector targets) — NOT loosen the threshold; also trimmed the graph height for margin. **(5) the per-round token guard** (the Bramble stale-timeout pattern) + **(6) the temp-file `cat` append** (NOT a `<<HEREDOC`). Architecture: a NEW clean `graph-it-core.js`/`graph-it-activity.js` (window.GraphIt*), **0 lines to any protected core / lcs-shell** (the only tracked edit = the `graph-it-activities.json` row in `activities.ts MANIFEST_FILES`); the gate trio `verify-graph-it-core.js` (MEASURED) + `_graphit-probe.js` + `local-test-graph-it.js`; full §A.13.62 DoD green (core gate + visual-qa 84 renders + probe 7 interacted states + local-test + personal Read 768/1024/360 + visual-critic PASS-WITH-NITS, the actionable thumbnail-readability nit fixed). No deploy (operator batches).

## Character #48 — Detective Dewey the magnifier ladybug & the field-guide page-furniture (table of contents / headings / glossary / icon-menu / index / captioned diagram) / the FICTIONAL creatures (gribbet/wuzzle/nim with frell/snood/tarn parts) / the Field Journal of keepsakes (Game #49 "Detective Dewey's Field Guide", 1.RI.5 know & USE text features to LOCATE key facts) — the ELA Reading-Informational strand's 1.RI.5 / text-features OPENER; the read-the-feature-to-locate game (the win routes THROUGH reading a feature, never picture-resemblance / word-match / prior knowledge)

**Detective Dewey the magnifier-toting ladybug naturalist keeps LOSING facts inside his field guides — and the child finds them by reading the book's own navigation tools** (a table of contents, section headings, a "Words to Know" glossary, an icon/menu strip, an index) **and its captioned diagrams of FICTIONAL creatures, never by guessing what the picture looks like or what they already know.** TWO verbs: **KNOW** (recognize what a heading/TOC/glossary/icon IS and is FOR — the "which tool do you open?" judgment) + **USE to LOCATE** (navigate a feature to a key fact BY ITS FUNCTION/MEANING). Every question is by function/meaning with the **answer-word ABSENT from the correct label** — "Which chapter is about how it can GLOW?" → the label is "Chapter 2: Frells" (no "glow"); the bridge "all about glowing" lives only in the chapter's gloss. **Build context (the gate-check):** a clean 1.RI.5 / text-features OPENER — UNOWNED at the activity level. (The framing "opens the ELA Reading-Informational family" was corrected: `nila-pond` already opened RI broadly via RI.K.2 [main-idea/key-details, K, read-ALOUD] — a DIFFERENT standard/grade/mechanic, the distinct neighbor; NOT a same-CCSS-code overlap → no operator ruling needed.) **EN-ONLY pilot** (the text IS the standard — native ensemble per locale at fan-out). **Moods/poses:** Dewey idle / happy-bob (a correct locate → a "FOUND IT!" stamp + a pressed keepsake drops into the Field Journal) / "let's re-read" scaffold pose. ~512², transparent, 12fps.

**Props / field-guide page (code-drawn DOM/SVG/CSS/WebAudio/TTS; FEATURES-AS-LIST is the canonical render — SR + mobile = ONE path; NO image-library 404):** **THE TABLE OF CONTENTS** (chapter title + one-line description), **THE SECTION HEADINGS** (heading + teaser), **THE "WORDS TO KNOW" GLOSSARY** (term + definition), **THE ICON/MENU STRIP** (fictional buttons — Glarn/Drext/Pled — each with a function blurb, NOT magnifier→search so it stays non-pre-knowable), **THE INDEX** (alphabetical line-leads), **THE CAPTIONED DIAGRAM** (a wordless FICTIONAL creature + a caption sentence "The snood helps it swim. The frell helps it glow. The tarn helps it dig." + tappable part labels). **THE MAGNIFIER** (Dewey's tool + the "you are here" highlight). **THE FIELD JOURNAL** (the non-numeric abundance vessel — a growing collage of pressed leaf/feather/pebble keepsakes; NEVER a count) + the coral **"FOUND IT!" stamp**. **ALL labels/captions/headings/TOC entries/glossary defs/questions are real DOM/SVG `<text>`** (11-locale; NEVER baked — the creature ART is wordless). **`FUNCTION_NOT_VISUALLY_OBVIOUS` is an art-affordance contract:** the CA5 brief renders all candidate parts at EQUAL visual salience with NO function-telegraphing (a "frell" must NOT obviously glow). CA5 worklist: Dewey (idle/bob/re-read — 3 poses) + the magnifier; ~8-10 FICTIONAL creatures (each with separable, equal-salience labeled part-layers — part-separability + no-telegraphing the only hard constraints); page-furniture frames (TOC / glossary box / heading panels / icon-menu / index card / parchment); the Field Journal (sparse→bountiful) + the "FOUND IT!" stamp. **The read-the-feature → found-it aha gets the most art budget.** **PER-LOCALE = EN pilot;** every caption/heading/glossary/TOC/question is a §21.3 new-content-type → §A.13.48 native-ensemble job per locale (the `QUESTION_WORD_NOT_IN_LABEL` contract is morphology-specific [German compounds, Finnish case], glossary alphabetization is per-language, the world-knowledge prior table is per-CULTURE, the §A.13.56 fixed-token trap).

**Build lessons recorded — (1) THE WORLD_KNOWLEDGE_SOLVER GATE ADVERSARY (the critic's deepest fix + a PROGRAM-WIDE finding):** the function→part mapping is often COMMON KNOWLEDGE (wings→fly, roots→drink, search→magnifier), so a knowledgeable child answers FROM THEIR HEAD, bypassing the feature — and `ANSWER_REQUIRES_TEXT` (a no-text solver) is structurally BLIND to it (a no-text solver ≠ a prior-knowledge solver). Defeated by NON-PRE-KNOWABLE-BY-CONSTRUCTION: **FICTIONAL part/term/section names** (frell/snood/tarn) so a real prior table (fly→wings, glow→light, meaning→glossary…) maps a function to a REAL part that is NEVER one of the fictional targets → the solver can't map → ≤chance. The gate now ships a STATEFUL `WORLD_KNOWLEDGE_SOLVER` carrying a real prior table; it scores 0% on the 12 USE rounds (the which-feature KNOW rounds, which ARE pre-knowable feature-purpose knowledge, are EXCLUDED + capped to a minority). **TECH-DEBT FILED (program-wide): the world-model adversary generalizes to any standard whose answer can live in the player's head — retrofit it alongside the Game-47 stateful + Game-48 enumerator solvers.** **(2) THE ANSWER-WORD-ABSENT-FROM-LABEL CONSTRUCTION:** the function word ("glow") is in the gloss/definition/caption (the bridge the child READS), NOT in the tappable LABEL ("Frell") — so a label word-match cheat fails (0%) while the read-the-gloss oracle wins (100%). Blanking the `functions` makes `expectedAnswer` null (the picture-proof). **(3) QUESTION-ONLY TTS (RI, not SL):** TTS speaks ONLY the question (the shell prompt's speaker); reading the feature IS the assessed cognition, so there is NO feature-text TTS (the local-test spy asserts no gloss is ever spoken) — pre-readers are out of scope for a Grade-1 *reading* game, stated not apologized for. **(4) the two personal-Read copy catches:** the TOC prompt "Which chapter is about glow?" was ungrammatical → "…about how it can glow?"; and the which-feature round showed TWO competing questions (the shell prompt + an in-stage "Which book tool would you open?") → the in-stage line became a HINT ("Think about what each book tool is for."). Both caught by the §A.13.62 personal-Read + critic, fixed, gate-revalidated. **(5) the §A.13.62 layout discipline:** the diagram skin (creature + caption + buttons) was the tall state — shrank the decorative creature + tightened spacing to clear the 320×640 fold; the target buttons carry a `fg-card` class so visual-qa measures the real controls (the graph-it appH-fallback lesson). **(6) the temp-file `cat` append** (NOT a `<<HEREDOC`). Architecture: a NEW clean `field-guide-core.js`/`field-guide-activity.js` (window.FieldGuide*), **0 lines to any protected core / lcs-shell** (the only tracked edit = the `field-guide-activities.json` row in `activities.ts MANIFEST_FILES`); the gate trio `verify-field-guide-core.js` (MEASURED) + `_dewey-probe.js` + `local-test-field-guide.js`; full §A.13.62 DoD green (core gate + visual-qa 84 renders + probe 8 feature states + local-test + personal Read 768/1024/360 + visual-critic PASS-WITH-NITS, the double-prompt + grammar nits fixed). No deploy (operator batches). **The conditional `caption-match` cog was DROPPED (the spec's "drop the trivial form") — it isn't cleanly gate-constructible without circular on-page knowledge; deferred to v2 with a proper mini-glossary-bridge design. 7 distinct cogs (6 USE non-pre-knowable + which-feature) clear the floor.**

## Character #49 — Rosa Raccoon & the gumball rhyme-wagon / the glass gumball globe + crank + chute / the rhyme-family "wagons" (sort bins) / the soundwave-ear cue (Game #50 "Rosa Raccoon's Rhyme Wagon", RF.K.2.a recognize & PRODUCE rhyming words) — the rhyme / phonological-awareness FAMILY-OPENER; the AUDIO-FIRST listening game (the FIRST library-image-consuming game in the cohort) — the win routes through the HEARD terminal rime, never picture / category / memory / SPELLING

**Rosa the carnival raccoon runs a gumball wagon whose machine is a "rhyme-EAR" — it doesn't care what the picture-tokens LOOK like or what they ARE, only whether their TAILS sound the same.** Feed it tokens whose names RING alike (bee/key, snail/whale) → it clinks and a gumball rolls into the big glass globe (the non-numeric abundance vessel). **AUDIO-FIRST** (Fork-1 operator ruling → the HONEST LISTENING GAME, "headphones recommended"): every picture token is TAP-TO-HEAR (TTS whole-word, NO audio-on-load); print is an INERT label (the plain word, **NO rime-highlight** — the critic's F-B cut; the rhyme is a SOUND). **The mechanic is trivial — the CONTENT CONSTRAINTS are the standard.** **Build context (the gate-check + the FEASIBILITY SCAN):** RF.K.2.a / rhyme was a clean UNOWNED PA-ladder gap (syllables/onset-rime-blending/beginning-sound all live; rhyme absent). The mandatory rhyme-inventory scan PASSED — 273 single-syllable concrete nouns verified in `image-vocabulary` ∩ `approved-words-en` ∩ COLOR assets. **OPERATOR RULINGS (this build):** (1) print-proof honest depth (use only the print-proof subset; document shallower shapes, never force-fill); (2) ALL 7 interaction shapes. **EN-ONLY pilot** (rhyme is the MOST language-bound skill — pairs don't translate; per-locale needs a native ensemble + native TTS + a per-locale feasibility scan; transparent orthographies [fi/es/it] make the different-spelling lever nearly impossible → DEFER). **Moods/poses:** Rosa idle paw-to-ear / listening / nod-yes / head-tilt re-listen. ~512², transparent, 12fps.

**Props / fair (Rosa + wagon + globe + machines = code-drawn DOM/SVG; the picture TOKENS are REAL `image-library-webp` color art — the cohort's FIRST library consumer):** **THE GUMBALL WAGON + GLASS GLOBE + CRANK + CHUTE** (a correct ring → a colored gumball rolls into the globe; full → Rosa cranks the handle + a cascade pours for the fair — the celebration; the abundance vessel, NEVER a count). **THE RHYME-FAMILY "WAGONS"** (the sort bins — 2 color-coded machines each anchored by a HEARD word "♪ Whale" / "♪ Bee", two-tap-place). **THE SOUNDWAVE / EAR CUE** (the rhyme-EAR ripple on tap; the rhyme RINGS BACK on a correct feed — staaar…caaar). **THE PICTURE TOKENS** = `/image-library-webp/themes/<themeDir>/<noun>@2x.webp` (snail/whale/bee/key/tree/scale/pear/knee/chair/pie/bear/tie/hair/trail — real color library art, served locally by the visual-qa + mobile harness; **content-sized tiles in a flex-wrap centred row** so the picture FILLS the card, not sparse). **Words ALWAYS real DOM `<text>`** (the plain inert label, NO rime-highlight). CA5 worklist: Rosa (4 poses) + the gumball wagon (wooden cart + brass + glass globe + crank) + 2-3 color-coded rhyme-family machines + the gumball cascade + a soft fair backdrop + the soundwave/ear cue (the rhyme-rings-back chime + the cascade get the most budget). **Token pictures STAY the library** (the game consumes it, no new token art); numbers/words stay DOM.

**Build lessons recorded — (1) THE PER-ROUND PRINT-MATCH GATE ADVERSARY (the critic's deepest fix + a PROGRAM-WIDE finding):** on the regular-spelling majority a letter-tail matcher PICKS THE CORRECT answer (cat/hat both `-at`), so a DECK-level "print-match fails" gate is false confidence while an early-reading child cheats every regular round — the cheat lives one level below the gate's resolution. Defeated DETERMINISTICALLY: two spelled signals per token — a hand-authored PHONOLOGICAL **`rimeKey`** (sound: bee/key both `IY`, snail/whale `EYL`) drives the oracle; the **`spelledRime`** (the vocab-phonics rime: cat→`at`, snail→`ail`, whale→`ale`) drives the print-matcher. **Every recognition round uses a DIFFERENT-SPELLING rhyme** (the correct rhymes but is spelled differently; no decoy shares the target's spelled-rime) → an exact-spelled-rime print-matcher NEVER lands on the correct → it guesses → ≤chance, **per round**. The gate ships a per-shape `printMatch` solver that produces the WRONG answer on 100% of rounds (judge/pick/odd/sort/chant/field/chain each defeated by construction). **TECH-DEBT FILED (program-wide): a deck-level constraint-ratio gate is BLIND to a per-round cheat — measure the cheat at the granularity the child plays; generalizes to every constraint-ratio gate (alongside the Game-47 stateful + Game-48 enumerator + Game-49 world-knowledge adversaries).** **(2) THE PRINT-PROOF INVENTORY NARROWING (the operator-ruled feasibility call):** the per-round print-defeat makes SAME-spelling families (cat/hat, car/jar) UNUSABLE (a print-matcher wins them) → the usable subset is the DIFFERENT-spelling families (ALE: snail/nail/trail-`ail` ↔ whale/scale-`ale`; IY: bee/tree/knee-`ee` ↔ key-`ey`; EHR: bear/pear-`ear` ↔ chair/hair-`air`; + the `ear`/EHR heteronym non-rhyme decoy) — pools shallower than the ≥7-deep ideal; operator ruled **honest depth, document-don't-force** (the §20.7 defer-not-force precedent). **(3) THE ASSET SUBSTRATE GATE (the FIRST library-image game):** the measured gate validates EVERY token against the REAL data — `word===IMAGE_VOCABULARY[noun].en[0]`, noun∈`approved-words-en`, single-syllable, `spelledRime===vocab-phonics rime`, a COLOR asset file exists (themeDir not `bw`) — all READ-ONLY (image-vocabulary + the library are NEVER touched, §20.5/§10.3). **(4) AUDIO-FIRST + the §A.13.55 no-audio-on-load:** TTS speaks only on tap (the inventory-404/startle hazard); the local-test spies on `LCSAudio.speak` (nothing on load, the word on tap). **(5) THE CONTENT-SIZED-TILE SPARSE FIX (the Echo-Grove/Mosaic lesson, re-applied):** a 48px image in a grid-STRETCHED (1fr) tile read as SPARSE (content ratio < 0.32) → switched to content-sized tiles HUGGING the image in a flex-wrap centred row → the picture fills the card. **(6) the chant-couplet ambiguity (a personal-Read/critic catch):** "…slide down a forest …" read as if "forest" were the rhyme slot → reworded so the blank is unambiguously LINE-FINAL ("…it rhymes with snail and with [trail]", the snail audio-scaffold). **(7) the per-round token guard + temp-file `cat` append.** Architecture: a NEW clean `rhyme-shop-core.js`/`rhyme-shop-activity.js` (window.RhymeShop*), **0 lines to any protected core / lcs-shell**, image-vocabulary + the library untouched (the only tracked edit = the `rhyme-shop-activities.json` row in `activities.ts MANIFEST_FILES`); the gate trio `verify-rhyme-shop-core.js` (MEASURED) + `_rosa-probe.js` + `local-test-rhyme-shop.js`; full §A.13.62 DoD green (core gate + visual-qa 72 renders + probe 8 shape states + local-test all 7 shapes + personal Read 768/1024/360 with real images + visual-critic PASS-WITH-NITS, the couplet fixed). **7 distinct shapes (4 RECOGNIZE: judge/pick/odd/sort + 3 PRODUCE: chant/field/chain); the per-voice TTS rime-fidelity audit is a curated-pool proxy here — the full audio-fidelity check is a runtime/CA5 concern.** No deploy (operator batches).

## Character #50 — Quill the hedgehog trader & the Mirror Market stall (the receive-only MIRROR-BOOK abundance vessel that clasps a facing-page spread per win) / the two relation-doors (Opposite | Same kind) / the tilted-seesaw scale / the backwards-bedtime-world scene (sun-up / lamp-on / door-open to flip) / the verb arrow-frames (push→ / pull←) (Game #51 "Quill's Mirror Market", K.L.5.b relate frequently-occurring verbs & adjectives to their OPPOSITES — antonyms) — the antonyms / word-relationships FAMILY-OPENER (K Language → Vocabulary); the ALL-CODE-DRAWN-GLYPH game (no library photos: swatch / arrow / motion-box / door / lamp / sun-moon / fill-jar / size-circle / thermometer / face) — a MEANING game (mute-safe, SR-first, color rounds also LABEL the color in text → not color-only). 7 genuinely-distinct child-ACTIONS (pick / generate / balance / verb / route / oddpair / scene). The win routes through the SAME-DIMENSION word at the OPPOSITE pole, DISCRIMINATED from a same-dimension SIBLING that is NOT the opposite (black→white NOT red; up→down NOT left; push→pull NOT lift) — never the same-kind sibling, never a friend/associate, never a mirror-image flip. Quill: 4 poses (greet / consider / clasp-the-book / nod). EN-ONLY pilot (the same-dimension-sibling FOIL is language-bound → per-locale native ensemble at fan-out).

## Character #51 — Skip the squirrel & the word-welding yard (the crane + claw / the two word-blocks welded at a coral seam / the Build Board diorama shelf — the abundance vessel where each built thing parks per win, never a count) (Game #53 "Skip's Word-Welding Yard", L.2.4.d use the meaning of the individual words to PREDICT the meaning of a compound word — birdhouse = a HOUSE for BIRDS) — the compound-word / morphology FAMILY-OPENER (Grade 2 Language → Vocabulary); a MEANING game (mute-safe, SR-first; the gloss + the two PART pictures carry it). 5 genuinely-distinct child-ACTIONS (predict = form→meaning on a NOVEL compound + the two part-pics, pick the composed gloss / ticket = meaning→form, pick the compound word / supply = the missing part-picture / head = the kind-of MEANING question never word-position / build = weld the two parts then resolve the meaning). The win routes through COMPOSING the meaning from the parts via the HEAD + the head-modifier RELATION on NOVEL compounds (both PARTS familiar, the WHOLE novel) — never retrieving a memorized whole, never reading a whole-compound picture (only the two PART pictures show), never gluing the part meanings (the same-head-WRONG-RELATION foil "a bowl made of fish" vs "a bowl for fish"). The PART pictures REUSE the real `image-library-webp` (color, §20.5); the compound + glosses are DOM `<text>` (head tinted teal). Skip: 4 poses (idle / welding / happy-build / gentle re-read). EN-ONLY pilot (compounds don't translate — full-fan Germanic+Finnish + DEFER Romance to a separate V+N-compound game; per-locale = native ensemble at fan-out).

## Character #52 — Sprout the bunny gardener & the irregular flower beds (L-shapes / staircases) / the moss-patch unit-SQUARES / the patches that KNIT into a bloomed meadow / the Meadow-Panorama strip (the abundance vessel where a flower joins per win, NEVER a count) (Game #54 "Sprout's Patchwork Meadow", 3.MD.C.6 measure area by covering a region completely with unit SQUARES — no gaps, no overlaps; area = the count of squares = the measure of the space; also_teaches 3.MD.C.5) — the AREA / measurement FAMILY-OPENER (Grade 3 Measurement & Data); a tap-to-place SVG/CSS covering game (mute-safe, SR-first; the ARIA grid + the area count carry it). 5 genuinely-distinct measurement child-ACTIONS (tile = cover an irregular bed from EMPTY, tap a cell to lay a patch / tap a covered cell to lift one / repair = fix a hidden bare GAP + a double-stacked OVERLAP shown as a coral "×2" cell / estimate = PREDICT the area before covering, the bed EMPTY so object-counting can't help / finish = complete a partly pre-tiled bed / build = make a bed of exactly N square units "for N carrots", the INVERSE — area as a SPACE target). The win routes through MEASURING THE SPACE: the beds are IRREGULAR (L-shapes, staircases → no length×width to multiply, no array to read — the 3.MD.C.7 + E13 fences); the count is DERIVED from the cover (never a stored area); and at the numeric-report modes the patches KNIT into one bloomed meadow before the area is stated, so a count-of-separated-visible-tiles solver (the K.CC.B.5 object-counting route) finds nothing to count and FAILS. NOTE — 3.MD.C.6 RECOGNITION is owned by the live `mosaic-menders` activity (Game #80, pick the mosaic with the same tile-count); gate-check fired → operator ruled "build #54 as the active PRODUCE game with the `conserve` (same-area-different-shape) mode CUT" so the producing game stays cleanly distinct from the recognizing one. §A.13.60 waiver: honest 5 actions + ≥7 distinct irregular regions (distinct shapes = distinct content), reshuffled order-only. Sprout: 2 poses (idle / happy). Direction-A (cream `#FBF3E4` / teal `#146B5E` the bed + patches / coral `#F2784B` Sprout + the overlap). EN-ONLY pilot (numbers carry it; the one per-locale care point is the inflected `areaUnitPhrase(n)` — Finnish partitive / Romance agreement, §A.13.56).

**A build lesson recorded — the FITS-via-recognized-control + the SPARSE-at-DESKTOP fix:** the pure-grid modes (tile/repair/finish/build) have NO `-choice` answer card and hide the shell Check until resolved, so `visual-qa-activity.js` fell back to the STRETCHED card height (`appH`≈949) at vh=900 → 4 false CUT-OFFs at 1024/1366×900. Fixed the Mosaic-Menders way: the interactive bed cell is `.pm-cand` (a harness-recognized control) so FITS measures the real content bottom, not the stretched card — NO gate threshold moved. Then the visual-critic (reading the FULL desktop sweep, the §A.13.62 step the automated SPARSE gate can't do) caught the board CLAMPED at 60px → a tiny cluster in a sea of cream on the wide desktop card (knock-on TINY on the "×2" + the number choices at 768); the automated SPARSE gate passed because it measures per-CELL fill (a solid cell IS its content), blind to "the whole board is small relative to the page card." Fixed by scaling the cell unit UP with the card (`--pm-cell: clamp(44px,16vw,88px)`, was max 60) + the choices (`clamp(...,72px)` / `clamp(1.45rem,5vw,2.1rem)`), phone (≤380) left exactly as-praised → critic CLEAN. The lesson: a measured per-element not-sparse gate does NOT see board-too-small-on-the-page; the human visual-critic on the desktop sweep is load-bearing for that class. (Grepped `patchwork`/`sprout`/`area-core` clear before writing; appended this character via a temp-file `cat`, the `<<HEREDOC` form silently fails in this shell.)

## Character #53 — Hazel the badger fence-keeper (RENAMED from the spec's "Bramble" — Bramble the badger is already char #46, Game #47 capacity) + Clover the sheep grass-planter / the paddock fences (rectangles + a C/L polyomino as SVG `<polygon>` with integer rectilinear vertices) / the "Fence rope: N" budget / the coral "?" missing-board badge / the boundary-ticks-vs-interior-squares unit tiles / the meadow-comes-alive strip (the abundance vessel where a flower joins per win, NEVER a count) (Game #83/#55 "The Mending Fences", 3.MD.D.8 solve problems involving the PERIMETER of polygons — the distance AROUND, distinct from area the fill) — the PERIMETER / boundary-measurement FAMILY-OPENER (Grade 3 Measurement & Data); a tap-to-place SVG game with DOM `<text>` side labels (mute-safe, SR-first; discrete-commit taps, NO drag / NO continuous-error signal at grade — the "gap" is hidden). 5 acts across the honest 3 graded cognitions (doubling-inverse / joint-ranking decoupling / around-vs-fill units): **mend-board** (given the rope-total P + EXACTLY ONE side, tap the missing board = ½P−side among plates that MANDATORILY include the P−side misconception foil) · **more-fence-or-grass** (two labelled fields, tap which needs MORE fence then which grows MORE grass — they rank OPPOSITELY) · **fence-it-or-plant** (tap the UNIT: edge-ticks vs interior-squares vs the border-ring foil) · **roll-reach** (does a roll of given length reach AROUND a labelled field?) · **same-area-diff-perim** (same grass, which needs more fence?). The win routes through MEASURING THE BOUNDARY: the doubling-inverse forces ½P−side (the naive "missing = P − the one shown side" lands on the present-and-wrong foil — OWNED SUBTRACTION killed); the decoupling is graded on the OPPOSITE orderings (AREA-COVARIATION killed) with ≥1 compact-but-high-perimeter C-field so "longest looks like most fence" misfires (the perceptual inversion); around-vs-fill grades the unit STRUCTURE not a keyword (the border-ring 2n+2m−4 foil present); answer DERIVED from sides, never stored. NOTE — this is the #83 "The Mending Fences" design (doubling-inverse + joint-ranking + around-vs-fill); the catalog also held a #55 "Woolly's Fence Line" (dual-graph border-laying) for the SAME standard — gate-check fired (two approved designs, one unowned standard, both pointing at perimeter-01.md which #83 had overwritten), operator chose build #83. Hazel: 2 poses (idle/happy). Direction-A (cream `#FBF3E4` / teal `#146B5E` fields + labels / coral `#F2784B` the "?" badge + ticks + Hazel). EN-ONLY pilot (numbers carry it; per-locale = the perimeter/around term + the around-vs-fill lexicon fence, §A.7 / §A.13.56).

**A build lesson recorded — the LANDSCAPE-ORIENT + SIDE-LABELS-FOR-SOLVABILITY + the l-closure-is-owned-subtraction findings:** (1) TALL fields (2×9, 4×8) EXPLODE vertically when width-scaled (height = width × aspect) → CUT-OFF at desktop; the fix is to author every field LANDSCAPE (w≥h) so a width cap bounds the height (perimeter/area are orientation-invariant). (2) The visual-critic (reading the desktop sweep, the §A.13.62 step the gate can't do) caught that the plain rectangles + roll-reach field gave NO numeric basis to solve (eyeball-only) → added per-pair side labels (one of each pair, opposite sides equal) so the perimeter is determinable; the C-mask guard-field stays label-free (visual perceptual-inversion) — the gates passed it but it wasn't solvable, the human catch. (3) A "?" missing-board glyph at small desktop size was MISREAD as "7" by two readers → made it an unmistakable coral circle-badge (a bare coral "?" is a real ambiguity, not a non-issue). (4) **l-closure (the spec's advanced act) was DROPPED**: a missing perimeter segment with all-other-segments-shown reduces to `missing = P − Σ(shown)` = OWNED SUBTRACTION (the very cheat the design fights); the honest non-owned version needs P + most runs hidden — too fiddly for a minority round → kept same-area-diff-perim as the one clean advanced act. (5) The spec's "GATED advanced tier" doesn't map to a free no-tracking activity (operator-locked NO progress tracking) → the advanced act ships as ordinary variety, not mastery-locked. (Grepped `mending-fences`/`fence`/`woolly`/`_fences` clear before writing; appended this character via a temp-file `cat`, the `<<HEREDOC` form silently fails in this shell.)

## Character #54 — Pesto the parrot soup-stall keeper & the broth-pot (the abundance vessel — a richer swirl + more steam-curls per win, NEVER a count) / the same-family spice-word jars / the customer degree-cue panel / the coral two-sided bounds (Game #56 "Pesto's Soup Stall", L.1.5.d distinguish SHADES of meaning among near-synonyms — INTENSITY [warm/toasty/hot/scalding] + MANNER [peek/glance/stare/glare]; also_teaches L.2.5.b) — the SHADES-OF-MEANING / vocabulary FAMILY-OPENER (the SAME-meaning-GRADIENT companion to the antonym game Game #51); a TEXT meaning-game (mute-safe, SR-first; the cue sentence + the word jars + the gauge carry it). 4 genuinely-distinct cogs (§A.13.60 waiver-at-4): **pick** (≥50% — read the customer's full degree-cue, hand Pesto the right-strength spice; the answer word is ABSENT from the cue → reason not copy) · **order** (tap a less-rote ladder weakest→strongest, a placed strip builds) · **bound** (stronger-than-X-weaker-than-Y, the bounds named in coral) · **manner** (choose the way-of-looking the scene describes). The win routes through IN-THE-MOMENT degree/manner reasoning: the **same-set invariant** (all jars one near-synonym family → membership gives no signal); **CONTEXT_KEYWORD_AMBIGUOUS** (every keyword → ≥2 answer-ranks across the corpus: soup→{1,4}, towel→{1,3}, voice→{1,3} → "baby→tiny" collocation recall fails); **NON_CANONICAL_ORDER** (order uses only rote:false sets [warm<toasty<hot, damp<wet<soaked], never the rote big<huge<gigantic → rank-recall fails); answer DERIVED from `rank`/`requiredRank`, never stored (manner's authored answerId is the legitimate semantic pairing). Warm degree-DIRECTIONAL nudge on wrong ("too fierce — gentler!" / "needs more pep — stronger!"), no advance. Pesto: 2 poses (idle/happy). Direction-A (cream `#FBF3E4` / teal `#146B5E` jars + pot / coral `#F2784B` crest + the bounds + the nudge). EN-ONLY pilot (the MOST language-bound L.5 strand — the keyword-AMBIGUITY corpus must be RE-authored per locale, not just the words; lead size+temperature, allow size-only locales).

**A build lesson recorded — the UNIFORM-JAR (no rank-size) + the dual-recall-gate:** (1) the spec floated word-weight "sized by strength" as a redundant cue, but **sizing the jars by rank would solve the ORDER mode by eye** (a size-proxy cheat) → shipped UNIFORM jars; the gradient is carried by the reasoning + the gauge, never the jar size. (2) The headline anti-cheat is the **DUAL recall gate** — the same-set invariant kills plain-synonym-match but leaves TWO recall routes (memorized RANK on familiar ladders + memorized context-KEYWORD collocation); a single familiar/world-knowledge solver catches only one. Fix = a SEPARATE adversarial solver per route (`FAMILIAR_RANK_SOLVER` defeated by NON_CANONICAL_ORDER; `CONTEXT_KEYWORD_SOLVER` defeated by CONTEXT_KEYWORD_AMBIGUOUS), EACH ≤chance, MEASURED in verify-shades-core. **LESSON (program-wide): when an answer can live in the child's head, enumerate EVERY recall route and gate each with its own solver + a structural invariant; the tell is a "fix" for one recall cheat that is ITSELF a different recall cheat (the pick-for-context fix that's keyword-collocation).** (3) Clean build — no duplicate-design fork, L.1.5.d unowned (the catalog's 1.L.5.b mis-tag re-tagged at build); a text meaning-game is layout-friendly (visual-qa + critic CLEAN first try; two optional polish notes [the order sub-line is small on desktop; bottom whitespace at 1024 = the standalone-stretch artifact, content-sized in production]). (Grepped `shades`/`pesto`/`_pesto`/`soup` clear; appended via a temp-file `cat`, the `<<HEREDOC` form silently fails in this shell.)

## Character #55 — Marina the lighthouse-cat & the Halfway Harbors of Tidecove (lantern-harbors = tens, lighthouses = hundreds; the halfway-buoy = the midpoint; the welcome tide-line = the band) / the boat-numerals docking for the night / the coastline constellation of safe lights (the abundance vessel — more harbor-lights per win, NEVER a count) (Game #81 "The Halfway Harbors", 3.NBT.A.1 use place value understanding to ROUND whole numbers to the nearest 10 or 100) — the ROUNDING / number-line-nearness FAMILY-OPENER (Grade 3 NBT); a SYMBOL-FIRST tap game (a bare numeral boat appears; the band/buoy reasoning is the child's, never pre-drawn; mute-safe, SR-first). 4 graded cogs (honest ~3 cognitions + §A.13.60 waiver): **set-band** ★ (tap a harbor's LOWEST and HIGHEST boat — the welcome band, e.g. the 100-lighthouse claims 50–149; the truncation-view 100/199 are present-and-wrong) · **halfway-buoy** ★ (tap the midpoint between two harbors) · **which-place** ★ (read which harbor-SIZE is lit — small lantern vs big lighthouse — and send the boat to the nearer home; the told place is UNDECLARED) · **catch-false-flag** (a docked boat: 168@100 / 42@50 / 40@40 — home or relocate?). THE CORE INSIGHT (the design's whole reason): forward rounding is a LEGITIMATE place-value procedure, so a SMART rote-rounder produces every forward answer with zero understanding — the grade therefore re-centers onto what the SMART rounder CANNOT produce (the band edges + midpoint = the inverse a one-way rule can't run backward; place-governance with the place undeclared; the meaning-traps).
